# Introduction to Computer Systems (ICS): past exam questions

Every question from the photographed sheets, books and PDFs in `ex-question/` that belongs to this subject. The same question asked in several exams, or printed in several sources, is shown once. For each question you get **where it was asked** (the exams, with names normalised in `../exam-tags.json`) and **where it was taken from** (each source document, linked to its transcription and image).

- **Distinct questions:** 487 (from 537 records: Written 309, MCQ 113, Viva 115)
- **Exams it appears in:** 117
- **Corrections to printed answers:** 9
- **How counts work:** "Asked in (N)" counts different government/public-sector recruitment exams (BCS, NTRCA, BPSC, state banks, public bodies) and BUET M.Sc. admission tests. Private-bank exams and compiled viva experiences are listed but not counted. Coaching suggestion sheets and model tests never add to the count.

## Coaching "standard pattern" topics (not counted)

Topics the Bit to Exabyte question book lists as its standard pattern for this subject (from `../Standard Pattern/`). They show what the coaching expects, not what was asked.

- 14. CPU functions; ALU vs CU vs registers; BIOS vs UEFI, firmware, cache vs RAM, DRAM vs SRAM notes — [Standard Pattern/01_super-ict-index-p1-q01-14.md](../Standard%20Pattern/01_super-ict-index-p1-q01-14.md)
- 73. Types of ROM; SRAM vs DRAM — [Standard Pattern/04_super-ict-index-p4-q64-85.md](../Standard%20Pattern/04_super-ict-index-p4-q64-85.md)
- 04. MS Excel: IF, SUMIFS, COUNTIFS, AVERAGEIFS, VLOOKUP, HLOOKUP, XLOOKUP, UNIQUE, sort and filter — [Standard Pattern/08_super-ict-index-p8-aptitude-bcs-sets.md](../Standard%20Pattern/08_super-ict-index-p8-aptitude-bcs-sets.md)

<a id="g0803"></a>

### Draw a server rack diagram showing horizontal and vertical scaling. <sub>G0803</sub>

*Written*

Draw a server rack diagram showing horizontal and vertical scaling.

- **Asked in (1):** RPGCL AM(ICT) 2022
- **Source:**
  - RPGCL Assistant Manager (ICT) (BUET) (2022), Q7 — [PETROBANGLA/03_sjf-scheduling-and-sha.md](../PETROBANGLA/03_sjf-scheduling-and-sha.md) · [image](../PETROBANGLA/03_sjf-scheduling-and-sha.jpg)
- **Printed answer:** Two labelled rack elevations (switches, KVM, servers, RAID arrays, UPS)
- **Correction:** Diagrams are generic rack layouts with no explanation; should show vertical scaling (bigger CPU/RAM in the same server) vs horizontal scaling (adding more servers/nodes behind a load balancer).

<a id="g0865"></a>

### Define a virtual machine with a diagram, explain how a VM works, and list its benefits. <sub>G0865</sub>

*Written*

Define a virtual machine with a diagram, explain how a VM works, and list its benefits.

- **Asked in (1):** BB AME 2023
- **Source:**
  - Bangladesh Bank Assistant Maintenance Engineer (BIBM) (2023-02-04), Q6 — [BANK/04_virtual-machine-and-data-centers.md](../BANK/04_virtual-machine-and-data-centers.md) · [image](../BANK/04_virtual-machine-and-data-centers.jpg)
- **Printed answer:** VM = virtual computer with own CPU/memory/storage on a hypervisor (VMM); diagram of single OS vs multiple guest OSes; benefits: isolation, easy maintenance, provisioning, recovery

<a id="g0866"></a>

### What are the challenges in optimizing the energy efficiency of data centres? <sub>G0866</sub>

*Written*

What are the challenges in optimizing the energy efficiency of data centres?

- **Asked in (1):** BB AME 2023
- **Source:**

*Viva*

UPS vs IPS—which is used in a Data Center and why?

- **Asked in (0):** no dated exam tag on the source
- **Viva mentions:** 1
- **Source:**
  - Bank IT viva (compiled from candidates' viva experiences) (viva), Viva p19 Q6 (+p20 Q10) — [PDF-TEXT/Viva Questions Extracted from Viva Experiences.md](../PDF-TEXT/Viva%20Questions%20Extracted%20from%20Viva%20Experiences.md#p19); [PDF-TEXT/Viva Questions Extracted from Viva Experiences.md](../PDF-TEXT/Viva%20Questions%20Extracted%20from%20Viva%20Experiences.md#p20)
- **Printed answer:** UPS (Uninterruptible Power Supply) is used in data centers because it provides backup power during electricity failure and prevents sudden shutdowns; IPS (Intrusion Prevention System) is a security device, not a power backup ...
- **Correction:** In a Bangladeshi power context 'IPS' means Instant Power Supply (inverter + battery), not Intrusion Prevention System. Expected answer: data centers use online UPS (zero/near-zero transfer time, clean regulated power, feeds generators); IPS has a switch-over delay and poorer regulation, so it is unsuitable for servers.

<a id="g0416"></a>

### What is a domain? How many types? <sub>G0416</sub>

*Viva*

What is a domain? How many types?

- **Asked in (0):** no dated exam tag on the source
- **Viva mentions:** 1
- **Source:**
  - NTRCA 17th registration viva 2023 (2023) (viva), Q5 — [VIVA/06_ntrca-topology-url-and-osi.md](../VIVA/06_ntrca-topology-url-and-osi.md) · [image](../VIVA/06_ntrca-topology-url-and-osi.jpg)
- **Printed answer:** A website's name/address on the internet; 5 main types: TLDs, sponsored TLDs, geographic TLDs, brand TLDs, new generic TLDs
- **Correction:** Muddled classification: "TLD" is the umbrella, not one of its own types. Standard IANA groups are generic (gTLD, e.g. .com), country-code (ccTLD, e.g. .bd), sponsored (sTLD, e.g. .edu/.gov) and infrastructure (.arpa); brand and new gTLDs are subsets of gTLDs.

<a id="g0705"></a>

### What is RAM? <sub>G0705</sub>

*Viva*

What is RAM?

- **Asked in (0):** no dated exam tag on the source
- **Viva mentions:** 1
- **Source:**
  - Bank IT viva (compiled from candidates' viva experiences) (viva), Viva p30 Q9 — [PDF-TEXT/Viva Questions Extracted from Viva Experiences.md](../PDF-TEXT/Viva%20Questions%20Extracted%20from%20Viva%20Experiences.md#p30)
- **Printed answer:** RAM (Random Access Memory) is temporary, volatile memory that stores data and programs currently being used by the CPU

<a id="g0706"></a>

### What is ROM? <sub>G0706</sub>

*Viva*

What is ROM?

- **Asked in (0):** no dated exam tag on the source
- **Viva mentions:** 1
- **Source:**
  - Bank IT viva (compiled from candidates' viva experiences) (viva), Viva p30 Q10 — [PDF-TEXT/Viva Questions Extracted from Viva Experiences.md](../PDF-TEXT/Viva%20Questions%20Extracted%20from%20Viva%20Experiences.md#p30)
- **Printed answer:** ROM (Read Only Memory) is non-volatile memory that permanently stores firmware or boot instructions

<a id="g0707"></a>

### Difference between RAM and ROM. <sub>G0707</sub>

*Viva*

Difference between RAM and ROM.

- **Asked in (0):** no dated exam tag on the source
- **Viva mentions:** 1
- **Source:**
  - Bank IT viva (compiled from candidates' viva experiences) (viva), Viva p31 Q11 — [PDF-TEXT/Viva Questions Extracted from Viva Experiences.md](../PDF-TEXT/Viva%20Questions%20Extracted%20from%20Viva%20Experiences.md#p31)
- **Printed answer:** RAM: Volatile, temporary storage; data is lost when power is off; ROM: Non-volatile, permanent storage; retains data without power

<a id="g0708"></a>

### What is EPROM? <sub>G0708</sub>

*Viva*

What is EPROM?

- **Asked in (0):** no dated exam tag on the source
- **Viva mentions:** 1
- **Source:**
  - Bank IT viva (compiled from candidates' viva experiences) (viva), Viva p31 Q12 — [PDF-TEXT/Viva Questions Extracted from Viva Experiences.md](../PDF-TEXT/Viva%20Questions%20Extracted%20from%20Viva%20Experiences.md#p31)
- **Printed answer:** EPROM (Erasable Programmable Read-Only Memory) is a type of ROM that can be erased using ultraviolet (UV) light and reprogrammed

- **Printed answer:** Alpha, Beta, Cupcake, Donut, Eclair, Froyo, Gingerbread, Ice Cream Sandwich, Jelly Bean, Marshmallow...
